### 6.2.11 颜色替换滤镜-ColorReplaceFilter

> 
> 功能：把图像中**指定源颜色**替换为目标颜色，可通过`tolerance`控制颜色容差，相近颜色也会被替换；保留原图alpha透明度。

1. 参数表（GUI检视器对应）

    | 参数 | 说明 | 默认 |
    | --- | --- | --- |
    | `originalColor` | 需要被替换的源颜色，Pixi数字格式 `0xff0000` | `0xff0000` |
    | `targetColor` | 替换之后的目标颜色 | `0x00ff00` |
    | `tolerance` | 颜色容差 `0‑1`；0=仅完全一模一样颜色；1=最大范围 | `0.2` |
2. 注意点

    1. `tolerance=0`只替换完全一模一样RGB；调高会把相近色彩一并替换，过高会出现大面积颜色错乱。
    2. **不修改alpha通道**；如果要做“颜色变成透明”，该滤镜做不到，需要使用`ColorMatrixFilter`或者自定义shader。
    3. 适合：简单单色背景替换；不适合复杂照片高精度抠图。
3. 代码示例

    ```html
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="zh-CN">
    <head>
      <meta charset="UTF-8">
      <title>ColorReplaceFilter 颜色替换滤镜</title>
      <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
      <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
      <style>
        .wrap {
          display: flex;
          gap: 20px;
          align-items: flex-start;
        }
        .panel {
          width: 360px;
          display: flex;
          flex-direction: column;
          gap: 12px;
          border: 1px solid #ccc;
          padding: 14px;
          border-radius: 6px;
        }
        .row {
          display: flex;
          gap: 8px;
          align-items: center;
        }
        label {
          width: 140px;
          font-size: 14px;
        }
        input[type="range"] {
          flex: 1;
        }
        input[type="color"] {
          width: 80px;
          height: 28px;
          border: none;
          cursor: pointer;
        }
        .val {
          width: 50px;
          text-align: right;
          font-size:13px;
        }
      </style>
    </head>
    <body>
      <div class="wrap">
        <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
        <div class="panel">
          <div class="row">
            <label>被替换源颜色</label>
            <input type="color" id="inpOriginal" value="#ff0000">
          </div>
          <div class="row">
            <label>替换目标颜色</label>
            <input type="color" id="inpNew" value="#00ff00">
          </div>
          <div class="row">
            <label>tolerance 颜色容差</label>
            <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.01" id="inpTolerance">
            <span class="val">0.20</span>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
            originalColor：要抠除/替换的原色<br/>
            targetColor：输出新颜色<br/>
            tolerance：容差越大，相近颜色也会被替换
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <script type="module">
        const app = new PIXI.Application();
        await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
        document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);

        const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
        const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
        sprite.scale.set(0.5);
        app.stage.addChild(sprite);

        function hexToPixi(hexStr) {
          return parseInt(hexStr.replace('#',''), 16);
        }

        const replaceFilter = new PIXI.filters.ColorReplaceFilter({
          originalColor: 0xff0000,
          targetColor: 0x00ff00,
          tolerance: 0.2
        });
        sprite.filters = [replaceFilter];

        const inpOriginal = document.getElementById('inpOriginal');
        const inpNew = document.getElementById('inpNew');
        const inpTolerance = document.getElementById('inpTolerance');
        inpTolerance.value = 0.20;

        inpOriginal.oninput = ()=>{
          replaceFilter.originalColor = hexToPixi(inpOriginal.value);
        };
        inpNew.oninput = ()=>{
          replaceFilter.targetColor = hexToPixi(inpNew.value);
        };
        inpTolerance.oninput = ()=>{
          const v = Number(inpTolerance.value);
          replaceFilter.tolerance = v;
          inpTolerance.nextElementSibling.textContent = v.toFixed(2);
        };
      </script>
    </body>
    </html>
    ```
4. 运行效果

### 6.2.12 卷积滤镜-ConvolutionFilter

> 
> 3×3卷积核滤镜；通过3行3列9个浮点数矩阵，实现**锐化、模糊、边缘检测、浮雕**等像素邻域运算。
> 原理：图像每个像素，和周围8邻域像素做加权求和输出新颜色。

1. 参数表

    | 参数 | 说明 | 默认 |
    | --- | --- | --- |
    | `matrix` | 3×3卷积核，一维数组长度固定为9 `[m0,m1,m2,m3,m4,m5,m6,m7,m8]` | `[0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0]` |
    | `width` | 被处理对象宽度，建议设为sprite实际宽 | `200` |
    | `height` | 被处理对象高度，建议设为sprite实际高 | `200` |

    > 
    > matrix排布顺序（行优先）

    ```
    m0 m1 m2
    m3 m4 m5
    m6 m7 m8
    ```

    `m4`是**当前中心像素权重**。

2. 常用预置卷积核（直接复制使用）

    ```js
    // 锐化
    const sharpen = [ 0,-1,0, -1,5,-1, 0,-1,0 ];

    // 模糊(均值)
    const blur = [1/9,1/9,1/9, 1/9,1/9,1/9, 1/9,1/9,1/9];

    // 边缘检测
    const edgeDetect = [-1,-1,-1, -1,8,-1, -1,-1,-1];

    // 浮雕
    const emboss = [-2,-1,0, -1,1,1, 0,1,2];
    ```

    > 
    > 注意：矩阵总和>1画面变亮；总和<1画面变暗；边缘检测总和=0会大量黑色。

3. 注意点

    1. **width、height必须与精灵实际渲染宽高保持一致**，如果写死200，大图卷积效果错乱、无效果、黑边。
    2. 边缘检测核总和等于0，输出像素大量负值，WebGL会clamp到0，图片大片黑色，属于正常现象。
    3. 只能固定 **3×3卷积核**，不支持5×5、7×7更大核；做大模糊不要用这个，改用`BlurFilter`。
    4. matrix数组必须恰好9个元素，多/少直接报错。
    5. 性能：每个像素采样9次纹理；大量sprite同时开启会消耗性能。

4. 代码示例

    ```html
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="zh-CN">
    <head>
      <meta charset="UTF-8">
      <title>ConvolutionFilter 卷积滤镜</title>
      <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
      <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
      <style>
        .wrap {
          display: flex;
          gap: 20px;
          align-items: flex-start;
        }
        .panel {
          width: 380px;
          display: flex;
          flex-direction: column;
          gap: 12px;
          border: 1px solid #ccc;
          padding: 14px;
          border-radius: 6px;
        }
        .row {
          display: flex;
          gap: 8px;
          align-items: center;
        }
        label {
          width: 140px;
          font-size: 14px;
        }
        select {
          flex: 1;
          padding:4px;
        }
        textarea{
          width:100%;
          height:80px;
          font-size:12px;
        }
      </style>
    </head>
    <body>
      <div class="wrap">
        <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
        <div class="panel">
          <div class="row">
            <label>预设卷积核</label>
            <select id="selKernel">
              <option value="off">关闭滤镜（原图）</option>
              <option value="sharpen">锐化 sharpen</option>
              <option value="blur">均值模糊 blur</option>
              <option value="edge">边缘检测 edge‑detect</option>
              <option value="emboss">浮雕 emboss</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label>matrix数组：</label>
            <textarea id="txtMatrix" readonly></textarea>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
            3×3卷积核，9个数字；修改预设切换效果<br/>
            width/height 需要匹配精灵尺寸，否则效果异常<br/>
            边缘检测总和为0，画面会大量黑色
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <script type="module">
        const app = new PIXI.Application();
        await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
        document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);

        const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
        const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
        sprite.scale.set(0.5);
        app.stage.addChild(sprite);

        // 预置矩阵，移除无效identity
        const kernelMap = {
          sharpen: [ 0,-1,0, -1,5,-1, 0,-1,0 ],
          blur: [1/9,1/9,1/9, 1/9,1/9,1/9, 1/9,1/9,1/9],
          edge: [-1,-1,-1, -1,8,-1, -1,-1,-1],
          emboss: [-2,-1,0, -1,1,1, 0,1,2]
        };

        let convFilter = null;

        const selKernel = document.getElementById('selKernel');
        const txtMatrix = document.getElementById('txtMatrix');

        function applyKernel(name){
          if(name === 'off'){
            sprite.filters = [];
            txtMatrix.value = "滤镜已关闭，显示原图";
            return;
          }
          const m = kernelMap[name];
          if(!convFilter){
            convFilter = new PIXI.filters.ConvolutionFilter({
              matrix: m,
              width: sprite.width,
              height: sprite.height
            });
          }else{
            convFilter.matrix = m;
            convFilter.width = sprite.width;
            convFilter.height = sprite.height;
          }
          sprite.filters = [convFilter];
          txtMatrix.value = JSON.stringify(m);
        }

        applyKernel('off');

        selKernel.onchange = ()=>{
          applyKernel(selKernel.value);
        };
      </script>
    </body>
    </html>
    ```
5. 运行效果

### 6.2.13 交叉阴影滤镜-CrossHatchFilter

> 
> 素描交叉排线效果；根据原图亮度生成斜向交叉网格线条，模拟钢笔画速写，输出黑白速写画风。
> 图像越暗，交叉线条越密集；亮部线条稀疏。


1. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>CrossHatchFilter 交叉阴影滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>启用 CrossHatchFilter</label>
	        <input type="checkbox" id="chkEnable">
	      </div>
	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        CrossHatchFilter：素描交叉排线<br/>
	        该滤镜无配置参数，勾选开启，取消显示原图
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);

	    const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
	    const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
	    sprite.scale.set(0.5);
	    app.stage.addChild(sprite);

	    const hatchFilter = new PIXI.filters.CrossHatchFilter();

	    const chkEnable = document.getElementById('chkEnable');
	    chkEnable.onchange = ()=>{
	      sprite.filters = chkEnable.checked ? [hatchFilter] : [];
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>
	```

2. 运行效果

### 6.2.14 复古CRT显示器滤镜-CRTFilter  

> 
> 模拟老式显像管显示器效果：扫描线、球面弯曲、噪点、边缘暗角（vignetting）。
> 支持动态修改全部参数，实例属性可直接赋值，不需要重建实例。

1. 参数表

	| 参数 | 说明 | 默认 |
	| --- | --- | --- |
	| `curvature` | 屏幕球面弯曲程度，越大弯曲越明显 | `1.0` |
	| `lineWidth` | 扫描线粗细 | `1.0` |
	| `lineContrast` | 扫描线对比度 0‑1 | `0.25` |
	| `verticalLine` | true=垂直扫描线；false=水平扫描线 | `false` |
	| `noise` | 噪点强度 0‑1 | `0.3` |
	| `noiseSize` | 噪点颗粒大小 | `1.0` |
	| `vignetting` | 暗角强度 0‑1 | `0.3` |
	| `vignettingAlpha` | 暗角透明度 | `1.0` |
	| `vignettingBlur` | 暗角模糊 | `0.3` |
	| `seed` | 噪点随机种子 | `0` |

2. 注意点

	1. 该滤镜**全部属性支持直接赋值修改**，不需要重建实例，区别于 CrossHatchFilter。
	2. `curvature=0` 关闭球面弯曲，画面平面；数值越大鼓包越明显。
	3. `noise=0` 完全关闭噪点；`lineWidth=0`关闭扫描线。
	4. 性能：全屏使用有一定片元开销，移动端注意。

3. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>CRTFilter CRT显示器滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 440px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 8px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 180px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	    input[type="range"] {
	      flex: 1;
	    }
	    .val {
	      width: 55px;
	      text-align: right;
	      font-size:13px;
	    }
	    .tip {
	      font-size:12px;
	      color:#555;
	      line-height:1.5;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>enabled 启用滤镜</label>
	        <input type="checkbox" id="chkEnable" checked>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>curvature 球面弯曲</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="2" step="0.05" id="inpCurvature">
	        <span class="val">1.00</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>lineWidth 扫描线粗细</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="4" step="0.1" id="inpLineWidth">
	        <span class="val">1.00</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>lineContrast 扫描线对比</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.02" id="inpLineContrast">
	        <span class="val">0.25</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>verticalLine 垂直扫描线</label>
	        <input type="checkbox" id="chkVertical">
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>noise 噪点强度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.02" id="inpNoise">
	        <span class="val">0.30</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>noiseSize 噪点颗粒大小</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0.5" max="4" step="0.1" id="inpNoiseSize">
	        <span class="val">1.00</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>seed 噪点随机种子</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="5" step="0.1" id="inpSeed">
	        <span class="val">0.50</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>vignetting 暗角强度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.02" id="inpVignette">
	        <span class="val">0.30</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>vignettingAlpha 暗角透明度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.02" id="inpVigAlpha">
	        <span class="val">1.00</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>vignettingBlur 暗角模糊</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.02" id="inpVigBlur">
	        <span class="val">0.30</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="tip">
	        CRTFilter：模拟老式CRT显像管显示器<br/>
	        扫描线、球面畸变、噪点、边缘暗角；支持直接修改属性
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>

	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ width:345, height:460 });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);

	    const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
	    const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
	    sprite.scale.set(0.5);
	    app.stage.addChild(sprite);

	    const crtFilter = new PIXI.filters.CRTFilter({
	      curvature:1.0,
	      lineWidth:1.0,
	      lineContrast:0.25,
	      verticalLine:false,
	      noise:0.3,
	      noiseSize:1.0,
	      seed:0.5,
	      vignetting:0.3,
	      vignettingAlpha:1.0,
	      vignettingBlur:0.3,
	      time:1
	    });
	    sprite.filters = [crtFilter];

	    const chkEnable = document.getElementById('chkEnable');
	    const inpCurvature = document.getElementById('inpCurvature');
	    const inpLineWidth = document.getElementById('inpLineWidth');
	    const inpLineContrast = document.getElementById('inpLineContrast');
	    const chkVertical = document.getElementById('chkVertical');
	    const inpNoise = document.getElementById('inpNoise');
	    const inpNoiseSize = document.getElementById('inpNoiseSize');
	    const inpSeed = document.getElementById('inpSeed');
	    const inpVignette = document.getElementById('inpVignette');
	    const inpVigAlpha = document.getElementById('inpVigAlpha');
	    const inpVigBlur = document.getElementById('inpVigBlur');

	    inpCurvature.value = 1.00;
	    inpLineWidth.value = 1.00;
	    inpLineContrast.value = 0.25;
	    inpNoise.value = 0.30;
	    inpNoiseSize.value = 1.00;
	    inpSeed.value = 0.50;
	    inpVignette.value = 0.30;
	    inpVigAlpha.value = 1.00;
	    inpVigBlur.value = 0.30;

	    chkEnable.onchange = ()=>{
	      crtFilter.enabled = chkEnable.checked;
	    };
	    chkVertical.onchange = ()=>{
	      crtFilter.verticalLine = chkVertical.checked;
	    };

	    inpCurvature.oninput = ()=>{
	      const v = Number(inpCurvature.value);
	      crtFilter.curvature = v;
	      inpCurvature.nextElementSibling.textContent = v.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    inpLineWidth.oninput = ()=>{
	      const v = Number(inpLineWidth.value);
	      crtFilter.lineWidth = v;
	      inpLineWidth.nextElementSibling.textContent = v.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    inpLineContrast.oninput = ()=>{
	      const v = Number(inpLineContrast.value);
	      crtFilter.lineContrast = v;
	      inpLineContrast.nextElementSibling.textContent = v.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    inpNoise.oninput = ()=>{
	      const v = Number(inpNoise.value);
	      crtFilter.noise = v;
	      inpNoise.nextElementSibling.textContent = v.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    inpNoiseSize.oninput = ()=>{
	      const v = Number(inpNoiseSize.value);
	      crtFilter.noiseSize = v;
	      inpNoiseSize.nextElementSibling.textContent = v.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    // seed 修改，直接操作uniforms.uSeed
	    inpSeed.oninput = ()=>{
	      const v = Number(inpSeed.value);
	      crtFilter.seed = v;
	      inpSeed.nextElementSibling.textContent = v.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    inpVignette.oninput = ()=>{
	      const v = Number(inpVignette.value);
	      crtFilter.vignetting = v;
	      inpVignette.nextElementSibling.textContent = v.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    inpVigAlpha.oninput = ()=>{
	      const v = Number(inpVigAlpha.value);
	      crtFilter.vignettingAlpha = v;
	      inpVigAlpha.nextElementSibling.textContent = v.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    inpVigBlur.oninput = ()=>{
	      const v = Number(inpVigBlur.value);
	      crtFilter.vignettingBlur = v;
	      inpVigBlur.nextElementSibling.textContent = v.toFixed(2);
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>
	```

### 6.2.15 圆点滤镜-DotFilter

`DotFilter` 属于 **pixi‑filters**，实现半色调网点效果，模拟印刷网点，将图像灰度转换为大小变化的圆点图案。

> 
> 原理：根据像素亮度计算网点半径；亮度越高圆点越大；使用旋转角度控制网屏方向。

1. 参数

	| 参数 | 中文 | 默认 | 说明 |
	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
	| `scale` | 网点缩放 | `1` | 网点整体尺寸，数值越大圆点颗粒越大；越小网点越细密 |
	| `angle` | 网屏旋转角度 | `5` | 单位弧度，控制网点阵列旋转方向；0为水平垂直对齐 |
	| `grayscale` | 灰度网点 | `false` | `true` 会先把图像转成灰度再生成网点；`false` 保留原图色彩 |


	> 
	> 性能提示：
	> 
	> - `scale` 过大画面会丢失细节，建议范围 `0.5‑4`；
	> - 该滤镜为单Pass shader，性能开销很低，适合大量实例使用。

2. 关键特性
	1. **效果由原图亮度驱动**
		- 亮区域网点更大，暗区域网点更小；纯黑区域网点几乎消失。
		- 只作用亮度信息，保留原图色彩。
	2. angle 使用弧度而非角度
		- 90°对应 `Math.PI/2`，180°对应 `Math.PI`，360°对应 `Math.PI*2`。
	3. 与其他滤镜区别
		- `DotFilter`：半色调印刷网点；
		- `PixelateFilter`：像素化方块马赛克。
	4. **`grayscale: true`**
		- 更像传统黑白印刷网点；
		- 画面只保留明暗层次，不保留颜色；
		- 适合做复古报纸、漫画、印刷海报效果。
	5.	**`grayscale: false`**
		- 保留原图颜色；
		- 网点会在彩色图像上生成；
		- 更适合做彩色印刷、杂志封面、潮流海报效果。

3. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>DotFilter 网点滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	    input[type="range"] {
	      flex:1;
	    }
	    .val{
	      width:60px;
	      text-align:right;
	      font-size:13px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>scale 网点缩放</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0.5" max="8" step="0.1" id="inpScale">
	        <span class="val">1</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>angle 旋转弧度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="6.28" step="0.05" id="inpAngle">
	        <span class="val">5</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>grayscale 灰度网点</label>
	        <input type="checkbox" id="chkGrayscale">
	      </div>
	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        DotFilter：半色调印刷网点滤镜<br/>
	        scale控制网点颗粒大小，angle控制网屏旋转，grayscale切换彩色/黑白网点
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);
	    const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
	    const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
	    sprite.scale.set(0.5);
	    app.stage.addChild(sprite);

	    const dotFilter = new PIXI.filters.DotFilter({
	      scale:1,
	      angle:5,
	      grayscale:false
	    });

	    sprite.filters = [dotFilter];

	    const inpScale = document.getElementById('inpScale');
	    const inpAngle = document.getElementById('inpAngle');
	    const chkGrayscale = document.getElementById('chkGrayscale');
	    const valScale = inpScale.nextElementSibling;
	    const valAngle = inpAngle.nextElementSibling;

	    // 初始化控件值
	    inpScale.value = dotFilter.scale;
	    valScale.textContent = dotFilter.scale;
	    inpAngle.value = dotFilter.angle;
	    valAngle.textContent = dotFilter.angle;
	    chkGrayscale.checked = dotFilter.grayscale;


	    inpScale.oninput = ()=>{
	      dotFilter.scale = Number(inpScale.value);
	      valScale.textContent = dotFilter.scale.toFixed(2);
	    };

	    inpAngle.oninput = ()=>{
	      dotFilter.angle = Number(inpAngle.value);
	      valAngle.textContent = dotFilter.angle.toFixed(2);
	    };

	    chkGrayscale.onchange = ()=>{
	      dotFilter.grayscale = chkGrayscale.checked;
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>
	```
4. 运行效果

    <iframe src="/note-front/animation/pixi/records/html/22.html" width="800" height="500"></iframe>
</div>

### 6.2.16 投射滤镜-DropShadowFilter

`DropShadowFilter` 属于 **pixi‑filters**，实现物体投影/外阴影效果，给精灵生成偏移模糊的投射阴影。

> 
> 原理：生成一层偏移、模糊的副本作为阴影层，混合到原图下方；可控制偏移、模糊、质量、颜色、透明度；`shadowOnly` 可只输出阴影隐藏原图。

1. 参数

	| 参数 | 中文 | 默认 | 说明 |
	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
	| `offset.x` | X偏移 | `4` | 阴影水平偏移，正数向右，负数向左 |
	| `offset.y` | Y偏移 | `4` | 阴影垂直偏移，正数向下，负数向上 |
	| `blur` | 阴影模糊 | `2` | 阴影模糊半径，越大阴影越柔和 |
	| `quality` | 模糊采样质量 | `3` | 模糊采样次数，越高阴影边缘越平滑，性能开销上升 |
	| `alpha` | 阴影透明度 | `0.5` | 阴影不透明度，0完全透明，1完全不透明 |
	| `color` | 阴影颜色 | `0x000000` | 十六进制RGB颜色值，默认黑色 |
	| `shadowOnly` | 仅输出阴影 | `false` | true隐藏原图，只渲染阴影；false原图+阴影同时显示 |

	> 
	> 性能提示：
	> 
	> 
	> - `blur`、`quality` 数值越大GPU开销越高，UI场景建议 blur ≤6，quality=2‑3；
	> - 阴影会向外溢出元素边界，容器需要预留边距，否则阴影会被裁切。
2. 关键特性
   1. **阴影在元素底层绘制**
      - 阴影是滤镜生成的后置副本，永远显示在原图下方；
      - 精灵紧贴画布边缘时，溢出部分会被裁剪。
   2. offset 是对象属性，读写：`filter.offset.x` / `filter.offset.y`；初始化可传对象 `offset:{x:4,y:4}`。
   3. `shadowOnly` 典型用途：单独提取阴影做分层渲染、调试阴影效果。
   4. 颜色使用 pixi 标准十六进制格式 `0xRRGGBB`，不要传入css字符串。
   5. 与其他滤镜区别
      - `DropShadowFilter`：向外投射投影；
      - `GlowFilter`：向外发光，无位置偏移。
3. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>DropShadowFilter 投影滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    canvas{
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	    input[type="range"] {
	      flex:1;
	    }
	    .val{
	      width:60px;
	      text-align:right;
	      font-size:13px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>shadowColor 阴影颜色</label>
	        <input type="color" id="inpShadowColor" value="#000000">
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>offset.x X偏移</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-20" max="20" step="1" id="inpOffsetX">
	        <span class="val">4</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>offset.y Y偏移</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-20" max="20" step="1" id="inpOffsetY">
	        <span class="val">4</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>blur 模糊半径</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="12" step="0.5" id="inpBlur">
	        <span class="val">2</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>quality 采样质量</label>
	        <input type="range" min="1" max="8" step="1" id="inpQuality">
	        <span class="val">3</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>alpha 透明度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.05" id="inpAlpha">
	        <span class="val">0.5</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>shadowOnly 仅输出阴影</label>
	        <input type="checkbox" id="chkShadowOnly">
	      </div>
	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        DropShadowFilter：物体投射外阴影滤镜<br/>
	        offset控制阴影偏移，blur/quality控制柔和度，shadowOnly可隐藏原图只看阴影；阴影溢出边界需要预留边距；使用基	础图形：矩形、圆形、三角形、五角星
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ 
	      width: 345,
	       height: 460 ,
	       backgroundColor: 0xffffff
	    });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);

	    const container = new PIXI.Container();
	    app.stage.addChild(container);

	    // 绘制基础图形：矩形、圆形、三角形、五角星
	    const rectGfx = new PIXI.Graphics();
	    rectGfx.rect(0,0,80,60).fill(0x3388dd);
	    rectGfx.position.set(40,40);
	    container.addChild(rectGfx);

	    const circleGfx = new PIXI.Graphics();
	    circleGfx.circle(0,0,35).fill(0xdd6644);
	    circleGfx.position.set(180,70);
	    container.addChild(circleGfx);

	    const triGfx = new PIXI.Graphics();
	    triGfx.moveTo(0,-40).lineTo(35,32).lineTo(-35,32).closePath().fill(0x22bb66);
	    triGfx.position.set(70,180);
	    container.addChild(triGfx);

	    const starGfx = new PIXI.Graphics();
	    const starR = 40;
	    starGfx.moveTo(0,-starR);
	    for(let i=1;i<10;i++){
	      const r = i%2===1 ? starR : starR*0.4;
	      const angle = (Math.PI*2*i)/10 - Math.PI/2;
	      starGfx.lineTo(Math.cos(angle)*r, Math.sin(angle)*r);
	    }
	    starGfx.closePath().fill(0xccaa22);
	    starGfx.position.set(200,220);
	    container.addChild(starGfx);

	    const dropShadowFilter = new PIXI.filters.DropShadowFilter({
	      offset:{x:4, y:4},
	      blur:2,
	      quality:3,
	      alpha:0.5,
	      color:0x000000,
	      shadowOnly:false
	    });
	    container.filters = [dropShadowFilter];

	    const inpShadowColor = document.getElementById('inpShadowColor');
	    const inpOffsetX = document.getElementById('inpOffsetX');
	    const inpOffsetY = document.getElementById('inpOffsetY');
	    const inpBlur = document.getElementById('inpBlur');
	    const inpQuality = document.getElementById('inpQuality');
	    const inpAlpha = document.getElementById('inpAlpha');
	    const chkShadowOnly = document.getElementById('chkShadowOnly');

	    const valOffsetX = inpOffsetX.nextElementSibling;
	    const valOffsetY = inpOffsetY.nextElementSibling;
	    const valBlur = inpBlur.nextElementSibling;
	    const valQuality = inpQuality.nextElementSibling;
	    const valAlpha = inpAlpha.nextElementSibling;

	    //初始化控件
	    inpShadowColor.value = '#' + dropShadowFilter.color.toString(16).padStart(6,'0');
	    inpOffsetX.value = dropShadowFilter.offset.x;
	    valOffsetX.textContent = dropShadowFilter.offset.x;
	    inpOffsetY.value = dropShadowFilter.offset.y;
	    valOffsetY.textContent = dropShadowFilter.offset.y;
	    inpBlur.value = dropShadowFilter.blur;
	    valBlur.textContent = dropShadowFilter.blur;
	    inpQuality.value = dropShadowFilter.quality;
	    valQuality.textContent = dropShadowFilter.quality;
	    inpAlpha.value = dropShadowFilter.alpha;
	    valAlpha.textContent = dropShadowFilter.alpha.toFixed(2);
	    chkShadowOnly.checked = dropShadowFilter.shadowOnly;

	    inpShadowColor.oninput = ()=>{
	      const hex = inpShadowColor.value.replace('#','');
	      dropShadowFilter.color = parseInt(hex,16);
	    };

	    inpOffsetX.oninput = ()=>{
	      dropShadowFilter.offset.x = Number(inpOffsetX.value);
	      valOffsetX.textContent = dropShadowFilter.offset.x;
	    };

	    inpOffsetY.oninput = ()=>{
	      dropShadowFilter.offset.y = Number(inpOffsetY.value);
	      valOffsetY.textContent = dropShadowFilter.offset.y;
	    };

	    inpBlur.oninput = ()=>{
	      dropShadowFilter.blur = Number(inpBlur.value);
	      valBlur.textContent = dropShadowFilter.blur;
	    };

	    inpQuality.oninput = ()=>{
	      dropShadowFilter.quality = Number(inpQuality.value);
	      valQuality.textContent = dropShadowFilter.quality;
	    };

	    inpAlpha.oninput = ()=>{
	      dropShadowFilter.alpha = Number(inpAlpha.value);
	      valAlpha.textContent = dropShadowFilter.alpha.toFixed(2);
	    };

	    chkShadowOnly.onchange = ()=>{
	      dropShadowFilter.shadowOnly = chkShadowOnly.checked;
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>

	```

### 6.2.17 浮雕滤镜-EmbossFilter

`EmbossFilter` 属于 **pixi‑filters**，实现图像浮雕效果，模拟灰度凹凸浮雕，通过卷积核提取图像边缘制造凸起凹陷视觉。

> 
> 原理：使用固定卷积矩阵对图像像素做邻域采样计算，强化明暗边缘产生浮雕凹凸观感；滤镜内部光照方向为硬编码，**不对外暴露角度参数**。

1. 参数

	| 参数 | 中文 | 默认 | 说明 |
	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
	| `strength` | 浮雕强度 | `2` | 控制浮雕凹凸效果强度；数值越大边缘凹凸越强烈；0时浮雕效果消失 |

	> 
	> 性能提示：
	> 
	> 
	> - `strength` 建议范围 `0‑5`，过高会画面噪点严重、色彩崩坏；
	> - 该滤镜为单Pass shader，性能开销低，可大量实例复用。

2. 关键特性

   1. **固定光照方向**
      - 光源方向滤镜内部写死，无法通过参数修改角度；
      - 依靠像素原有明暗差异生成凹凸浮雕观感。
   2. 强度对画面的影响
      - strength=0：滤镜无效果，原图原样输出；
      - strength增大：边缘对比变强，凹凸感加重；
      - strength过大：画面出现杂色、色块撕裂，建议不要超过5。
   3. 与其他滤镜区别
      - `EmbossFilter`：卷积实现浮雕凹凸效果；
      - `DropShadowFilter`：物体外投影阴影滤镜。
   4. 适合场景
      - 图片、文字、图形做复古浮雕质感；
      - UI元素模拟压印、雕刻视觉；
      - 适合灰度图，彩色图像会出现色彩偏移。

3. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">

	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>EmbossFilter 浮雕滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }

	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }

	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }

	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }

	    input[type="range"] {
	      flex: 1;
	    }

	    .val {
	      width: 60px;
	      text-align: right;
	      font-size: 13px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>

	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>strength 浮雕强度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="5" step="0.1" id="inpStrength">
	        <span class="val">2</span>
	      </div>
	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        EmbossFilter：图像浮雕滤镜<br />
	        strength控制浮雕凹凸强度；滤镜无角度参数，光照方向内置固定
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);
	    const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
	    const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
	    sprite.scale.set(0.5);
	    app.stage.addChild(sprite);
	    const embossFilter = new PIXI.filters.EmbossFilter(2);
	    sprite.filters = [embossFilter];

	    const inpStrength = document.getElementById('inpStrength');
	    const valStrength = inpStrength.nextElementSibling;

	    // 初始化控件值
	    inpStrength.value = embossFilter.strength;
	    valStrength.textContent = embossFilter.strength;

	    inpStrength.oninput = () => {
	      embossFilter.strength = Number(inpStrength.value);
	      valStrength.textContent = embossFilter.strength.toFixed(1);
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>

	</html>
	```

### 6.2.18 花屏故障滤镜-GlitchFilter

`GlitchFilter` 属于 **pixi‑filters**，实现数字故障艺术效果，对图像做切片错位偏移，可搭配RGB通道分离，模拟信号干扰、画面撕裂花屏故障效果。

> 
> 原理：将图像切分为若干水平切片，按照随机规则对切片做角度方向偏移；支持RGB三通道独立偏移；偏移空缺区域支持多种填充模式；seed控制随机噪声种子，相同seed输出固定故障图案。

1. 参数

	| 参数 | 中文 | 默认 | 说明 |
	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
	| `slices` | 切片数量 | `5` | 图像被分割的切片条数；数值越大切片越细碎，故障细节越多 |
	| `offset` | 最大偏移量 | `100` | 切片最大错位像素距离；数值越大画面撕裂位移越剧烈 |
	| `direction` | 偏移角度 | `0` | 单位角度，切片偏移的朝向；0为水平偏移，90为垂直偏移 |
	| `average` | 均等切片 | `false` | `true`切片高度尽量均等；`false`切片高度随机差异大，故障更杂乱 |
	| `seed` | 随机种子 | `0` | 故障随机种子；相同seed得到完全一致的故障图案；变更seed刷新随机效果 |
	| `fillMode` | 空缺填充模式 | `0` | 切片偏移后空白区域填充：0透明、1原图、2循环、3截取、4镜像 |
	| `red` | R通道偏移 | `{x:0,y:0}` | 红色通道额外偏移 {x,y}，实现RGB色散分离故障 |
	| `green` | G通道偏移 | `{x:0,y:0}` | 绿色通道额外偏移 {x,y} |
	| `blue` | B通道偏移 | `{x:0,y:0}` | 蓝色通道额外偏移 {x,y} |

	> 
	> 性能提示：
	> 
	> 
	> - `slices`不建议超过30，切片过多会增大GPU开销；
	> - `offset`建议范围 `0‑120`，数值过大会画面完全撕裂无法辨认原图；
	> - 静态使用设置固定seed；做动画故障时，每帧修改seed调用`filter.refresh()`刷新故障图案。

2. 关键特性

   1. **切片错位故障核心**
      - 将图像横向切条，每条切片独立偏移，制造画面撕裂、信号干扰观感；
      - direction参数可以切换水平故障 / 垂直故障效果。
   2. fillMode五种填充模式
      - `0 TRANSPARENT`：偏移露出透明背景；
      - `1 ORIGINAL`：空缺处使用原图内容填充；
      - `2 LOOP`：纹理循环平铺填充；
      - `3 CLAMP`：边缘像素拉伸填充；
      - `4 MIRROR`：镜像翻转填充。
   3. RGB通道色散分离
      - red/green/blue设置xy偏移，产生彩边色散故障；全部为0则关闭RGB分离；
      - 例如 `red:{x:-5,y:0}, blue:{x:5,y:0}` 经典左右RGB色散。
   4. seed与refresh()
      - seed=0每次渲染随机；指定数字seed得到固定故障样式；
      - 修改seed后调用 `glitchFilter.refresh()` 重新生成切片随机数据。
   5. 与其他滤镜区别
      - `GlitchFilter`：切片错位+RGB色散，数字信号故障；
      - `RGBSplitFilter`：仅简单三通道偏移，无切片撕裂；
      - `PixelateFilter`：方块马赛克像素化，无切片错位。
   6. 适用场景
      - 赛博朋克、故障风UI、视频花屏模拟；
      - 游戏受击、信号干扰、转场特效；
      - 图片、精灵、文字、容器均可挂载。

3. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>GlitchFilter 故障艺术滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	    input[type="range"] {
	      flex:1;
	    }
	    .val{
	      width:60px;
	      text-align:right;
	      font-size:13px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>slices 切片数量</label>
	        <input type="range" min="1" max="30" step="1" id="inpSlices">
	        <span class="val">5</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>offset 最大偏移</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="120" step="1" id="inpOffset">
	        <span class="val">100</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>direction 偏移角度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="360" step="1" id="inpDirection">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>average 均等切片</label>
	        <input type="checkbox" id="chkAverage">
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>seed随机种子</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="100" step="1" id="inpSeed">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>fillMode 填充模式</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="4" step="1" id="inpFillMode">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>red.x R通道X偏移</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-30" max="30" step="1" id="inpRedX">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>red.y R通道Y偏移</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-30" max="30" step="1" id="inpRedY">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>green.x G通道X偏移</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-30" max="30" step="1" id="inpGreenX">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>green.y G通道Y偏移</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-30" max="30" step="1" id="inpGreenY">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>blue.x B通道X偏移</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-30" max="30" step="1" id="inpBlueX">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>blue.y B通道Y偏移</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-30" max="30" step="1" id="inpBlueY">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        GlitchFilter：数字故障艺术滤镜<br/>
	        slices切片数量，offset控制撕裂强度，direction控制偏移朝向；修改seed/fillMode/通道偏移后调用refresh刷新
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);
	    const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
	    const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
	    sprite.scale.set(0.5);
	    app.stage.addChild(sprite);

	    const glitchFilter = new PIXI.filters.GlitchFilter({
	      slices:5,
	      offset:100,
	      direction:0,
	      average:false,
	      seed:0,
	      fillMode:0,
	      red:{x:0,y:0},
	      green:{x:0,y:0},
	      blue:{x:0,y:0}
	    });
	    sprite.filters = [glitchFilter];

	    const inpSlices = document.getElementById('inpSlices');
	    const inpOffset = document.getElementById('inpOffset');
	    const inpDirection = document.getElementById('inpDirection');
	    const chkAverage = document.getElementById('chkAverage');
	    const inpSeed = document.getElementById('inpSeed');
	    const inpFillMode = document.getElementById('inpFillMode');
	    const inpRedX = document.getElementById('inpRedX');
	    const inpRedY = document.getElementById('inpRedY');
	    const inpGreenX = document.getElementById('inpGreenX');
	    const inpGreenY = document.getElementById('inpGreenY');
	    const inpBlueX = document.getElementById('inpBlueX');
	    const inpBlueY = document.getElementById('inpBlueY');

	    const valSlices = inpSlices.nextElementSibling;
	    const valOffset = inpOffset.nextElementSibling;
	    const valDirection = inpDirection.nextElementSibling;
	    const valSeed = inpSeed.nextElementSibling;
	    const valFillMode = inpFillMode.nextElementSibling;
	    const valRedX = inpRedX.nextElementSibling;
	    const valRedY = inpRedY.nextElementSibling;
	    const valGreenX = inpGreenX.nextElementSibling;
	    const valGreenY = inpGreenY.nextElementSibling;
	    const valBlueX = inpBlueX.nextElementSibling;
	    const valBlueY = inpBlueY.nextElementSibling;

	    //初始化控件
	    inpSlices.value = glitchFilter.slices;
	    valSlices.textContent = glitchFilter.slices;
	    inpOffset.value = glitchFilter.offset;
	    valOffset.textContent = glitchFilter.offset;
	    inpDirection.value = glitchFilter.direction;
	    valDirection.textContent = glitchFilter.direction;
	    chkAverage.checked = glitchFilter.average;
	    inpSeed.value = glitchFilter.seed;
	    valSeed.textContent = glitchFilter.seed;
	    inpFillMode.value = glitchFilter.fillMode;
	    valFillMode.textContent = glitchFilter.fillMode;

	    inpRedX.value = glitchFilter.red.x;
	    valRedX.textContent = glitchFilter.red.x;
	    inpRedY.value = glitchFilter.red.y;
	    valRedY.textContent = glitchFilter.red.y;
	    inpGreenX.value = glitchFilter.green.x;
	    valGreenX.textContent = glitchFilter.green.x;
	    inpGreenY.value = glitchFilter.green.y;
	    valGreenY.textContent = glitchFilter.green.y;
	    inpBlueX.value = glitchFilter.blue.x;
	    valBlueX.textContent = glitchFilter.blue.x;
	    inpBlueY.value = glitchFilter.blue.y;
	    valBlueY.textContent = glitchFilter.blue.y;

	    inpSlices.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.slices = Number(inpSlices.value);
	      valSlices.textContent = glitchFilter.slices;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    inpOffset.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.offset = Number(inpOffset.value);
	      valOffset.textContent = glitchFilter.offset;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    inpDirection.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.direction = Number(inpDirection.value);
	      valDirection.textContent = glitchFilter.direction;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    chkAverage.onchange = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.average = chkAverage.checked;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    inpSeed.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.seed = Number(inpSeed.value);
	      valSeed.textContent = glitchFilter.seed;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    inpFillMode.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.fillMode = Number(inpFillMode.value);
	      valFillMode.textContent = glitchFilter.fillMode;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    inpRedX.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.red.x = Number(inpRedX.value);
	      valRedX.textContent = glitchFilter.red.x;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    inpRedY.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.red.y = Number(inpRedY.value);
	      valRedY.textContent = glitchFilter.red.y;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    inpGreenX.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.green.x = Number(inpGreenX.value);
	      valGreenX.textContent = glitchFilter.green.x;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    inpGreenY.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.green.y = Number(inpGreenY.value);
	      valGreenY.textContent = glitchFilter.green.y;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    inpBlueX.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.blue.x = Number(inpBlueX.value);
	      valBlueX.textContent = glitchFilter.blue.x;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	    inpBlueY.oninput = ()=>{
	      glitchFilter.blue.y = Number(inpBlueY.value);
	      valBlueY.textContent = glitchFilter.blue.y;
	      glitchFilter.refresh();
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>
	```

### 6.2.19 辉光滤镜-GlowFilter

> 
> 不再使用图片Sprite，使用PIXI.Graphics绘制基础图形集合挂载滤镜，UI面板保留全部参数包含`alpha`。

`GlowFilter` 属于 **pixi‑filters**，实现外发光辉光效果，对物体边缘做高斯模糊向外扩散，模拟发光、光晕、霓虹辉光特效。

> 
> 原理：对物体alpha边缘做多次高斯模糊采样，向外扩张半透明发光层，叠加到原图之上；可控制发光颜色、扩张范围、透明度与模糊质量。

1. 参数

	| 参数 | 中文 | 默认 | 说明 |
	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
	| `distance` | 发光距离 | `10` | 辉光向外扩散像素距离；数值越大光晕铺得越远 |
	| `outerStrength` | 外发光强度 | `4` | 外部光晕亮度倍率；越高发光越亮，过高会出现曝光泛白 |
	| `innerStrength` | 内发光强度 | `0` | 向内发光强度，0关闭内发光；大于0向图形内部渲染光晕 |
	| `color` | 发光颜色 | `0xFFFFFF` | 十六进制RGB发光色值，白色`0xFFFFFF`、蓝色`0x0088FF` |
	| `alpha` | 发光透明度 | `1` | 光晕整体不透明度；0光晕完全消失，1完全不透明 |
	| `quality` | 模糊质量 | `0.1` | 辉光采样质量，范围0‑1；越高光晕越平滑，性能开销上升 |
	| `knockout` | 抠除原图 | `false` | `true`只保留发光层，隐藏原始图形本体；`false`原图+光晕一起渲染 |

	> 
	> 性能提示：
	> 
	> 
	> - `distance` 建议范围 `0‑30`，距离越大GPU渲染开销越高；
	> - `quality` 0.3‑0.6为性能与效果平衡点，不需要极致平滑不要设置到1；
	> - 大量实例使用时尽量复用同一个filter对象，减少shader切换开销。

2. 关键特性
   1. **外发光与内发光双模式**
      - outerStrength：控制物体外面一圈光晕；
      - innerStrength：向图形轮廓内侧做辉光，适合霓虹描边内侧泛光。
   2. knockout抠图模式
      - `knockout:true`：仅输出发光光晕，原始图形内容消失；适合单独拿光晕做叠加层。
   3. color十六进制颜色
      - 只接受数字十六进制，不要传入字符串；例红色 `0xFF3333`。
   4. alpha控制整体光晕透明度
      - 只作用辉光，不会修改图形本体透明度。
   5. quality对噪点影响
      - quality过低光晕会出现锯齿方块；提高quality得到柔和光晕，但会增加绘制pass。
   6. 与其他滤镜区别
      - `GlowFilter`：向外/向内扩散辉光光晕；
      - `DropShadowFilter`：生成实体投影阴影；
      - `BlurFilter`：整体全图模糊，不区分边缘发光。
   7. 适合场景
      - 霓虹UI、选中高亮、技能光效、图形发光；
      - 图标光晕、粒子发光叠加、赛博朋克发光元素。
3. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>GlowFilter 辉光滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    canvas{
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	    input[type="range"] {
	      flex:1;
	    }
	    .val{
	      width:60px;
	      text-align:right;
	      font-size:13px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>glowColor 发光颜色</label>
	        <input type="color" id="inpGlowColor" value="#000000">
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>distance 发光距离</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="30" step="1" id="inpDistance">
	        <span class="val">10</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>outerStrength 外发光</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="10" step="0.2" id="inpOuterStrength">
	        <span class="val">4</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>innerStrength 内发光</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="10" step="0.2" id="inpInnerStrength">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>alpha 发光透明度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.05" id="inpAlpha">
	        <span class="val">1</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>quality 模糊质量</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.05" id="inpQuality">
	        <span class="val">0.1</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>knockout 抠除原图</label>
	        <input type="checkbox" id="chkKnockout">
	      </div>
	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        GlowFilter：辉光发光滤镜<br/>
	        distance控制光晕扩散范围，outerStrength外发光亮度，innerStrength向内辉光，alpha控制光晕整体透明度；使用	基础图形：矩形、圆形、三角形、五角星
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({
	      width: 345,
	      height: 460,
	      backgroundColor: 0xffffff
	    });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);

	    const container = new PIXI.Container();
	    app.stage.addChild(container);

	    //绘制基础图形：矩形、圆形、三角形、五角星
	    const rectGfx = new PIXI.Graphics();
	    rectGfx.rect(0,0,80,60).fill(0x3388dd);
	    rectGfx.position.set(40,40);
	    container.addChild(rectGfx);

	    const circleGfx = new PIXI.Graphics();
	    circleGfx.circle(0,0,35).fill(0xdd6644);
	    circleGfx.position.set(180,70);
	    container.addChild(circleGfx);

	    const triGfx = new PIXI.Graphics();
	    triGfx.moveTo(0,-40).lineTo(35,32).lineTo(-35,32).closePath().fill(0x22bb66);
	    triGfx.position.set(70,180);
	    container.addChild(triGfx);

	    const starGfx = new PIXI.Graphics();
	    const starR = 40;
	    starGfx.moveTo(0,-starR);
	    for(let i=1;i<10;i++){
	      const r = i%2===1 ? starR : starR*0.4;
	      const angle = (Math.PI*2*i)/10 - Math.PI/2;
	      starGfx.lineTo(Math.cos(angle)*r, Math.sin(angle)*r);
	    }
	    starGfx.closePath().fill(0xccaa22);
	    starGfx.position.set(200,220);
	    container.addChild(starGfx);

	    const glowFilter = new PIXI.filters.GlowFilter({
	      distance:10,
	      outerStrength:4,
	      innerStrength:0,
	      alpha:1,
	      color:0x000000,
	      quality:0.1,
	      knockout:false
	    });
	    container.filters = [glowFilter];

	    const inpGlowColor = document.getElementById('inpGlowColor');
	    const inpDistance = document.getElementById('inpDistance');
	    const inpOuterStrength = document.getElementById('inpOuterStrength');
	    const inpInnerStrength = document.getElementById('inpInnerStrength');
	    const inpAlpha = document.getElementById('inpAlpha');
	    const inpQuality = document.getElementById('inpQuality');
	    const chkKnockout = document.getElementById('chkKnockout');

	    const valDistance = inpDistance.nextElementSibling;
	    const valOuterStrength = inpOuterStrength.nextElementSibling;
	    const valInnerStrength = inpInnerStrength.nextElementSibling;
	    const valAlpha = inpAlpha.nextElementSibling;
	    const valQuality = inpQuality.nextElementSibling;

	    //初始化控件
	    inpGlowColor.value = '#' + glowFilter.color.toString(16).padStart(6,'0');
	    inpDistance.value = glowFilter.distance;
	    valDistance.textContent = glowFilter.distance;
	    inpOuterStrength.value = glowFilter.outerStrength;
	    valOuterStrength.textContent = glowFilter.outerStrength.toFixed(1);
	    inpInnerStrength.value = glowFilter.innerStrength;
	    valInnerStrength.textContent = glowFilter.innerStrength.toFixed(1);
	    inpAlpha.value = glowFilter.alpha;
	    valAlpha.textContent = glowFilter.alpha.toFixed(2);
	    inpQuality.value = glowFilter.quality;
	    valQuality.textContent = glowFilter.quality.toFixed(2);
	    chkKnockout.checked = glowFilter.knockout;

	    inpGlowColor.oninput = ()=>{
	      const hex = inpGlowColor.value.replace('#','');
	      glowFilter.color = parseInt(hex,16);
	    };
	    inpDistance.oninput = ()=>{
	      glowFilter.distance = Number(inpDistance.value);
	      valDistance.textContent = glowFilter.distance;
	    };
	    inpOuterStrength.oninput = ()=>{
	      glowFilter.outerStrength = Number(inpOuterStrength.value);
	      valOuterStrength.textContent = glowFilter.outerStrength.toFixed(1);
	    };
	    inpInnerStrength.oninput = ()=>{
	      glowFilter.innerStrength = Number(inpInnerStrength.value);
	      valInnerStrength.textContent = glowFilter.innerStrength.toFixed(1);
	    };
	    inpAlpha.oninput = ()=>{
	      glowFilter.alpha = Number(inpAlpha.value);
	      valAlpha.textContent = glowFilter.alpha.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    inpQuality.oninput = ()=>{
	      glowFilter.quality = Number(inpQuality.value);
	      valQuality.textContent = glowFilter.quality.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    chkKnockout.onchange = ()=>{
	      glowFilter.knockout = chkKnockout.checked;
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>
	```

### 6.2.20 神光滤镜-GodrayFilter


`GodrayFilter` 属于 **pixi‑filters**，模拟丁达尔效应、太阳光透过云层的体积光束效果，分为平行光束与汇聚放射光束两种模式。

> 
> 原理：基于分形噪声采样，模拟介质中光线散射；`parallel=true`为平行太阳光；`parallel=false`从`center`点向外放射光束；修改`time`可实现光束流动动画。

1. 参数| 参数 | 中文 | 默认 | 说明 |

	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
	| `angle` | 光线角度 | `30` | 单位角度；parallel=true生效，控制平行光束倾斜方向，0垂直向下，90水平向右 |
	| `gain` | 光线强度 | `0.5` | 光束整体亮度；数值越大光束越亮，过高容易过曝发白 |
	| `lacunarity` | 噪声密度 | `2.5` | 分形噪声密度；数值越大光束缝隙越多、光束越细碎斑驳 |
	| `parallel` | 平行光束 | `true` | true平行太阳光(用angle)；false放射汇聚光(用center) |
	| `time` | 时间因子 | `0` | 动画时间；每一帧递增time，光束产生流动飘动效果 |
	| `center` | 放射光源中心点 | `{x:0,y:0}` | parallel=false生效，光束放射原点，使用画布相对坐标 |
	| `alpha` | 光束透明度 | `1` | 光束整体不透明度；0完全透明，1完全显示 |

	> 
	> 性能提示

	- 该滤镜是后处理噪点采样，CPU开销极低，主要消耗GPU；
	- 做动画：`app.ticker.add(()=> godrayFilter.time +=0.015)` 实现光束流动；
	- `parallel:false`放射模式，center坐标是滤镜容器的局部坐标。

2. 关键特性

	1. **两种光束模式**
		- 平行模式：`parallel:true`，模拟高空太阳光，由`angle`控制光束倾斜角度；
		- 放射模式：`parallel:false`，光束从`center`向外发散，模拟灯泡、太阳放射光效。
	2. time流动动画
		- time持续增加，内部噪声纹理滚动，光束产生云层流动、光线飘动效果。
	3. lacunarity噪声控制
		- 数值小：光束粗、缝隙少；数值大：光束细碎、斑驳、缝隙多。
	4. 与其他滤镜区别
		- `GodrayFilter`：体积光束丁达尔，叠加在整个渲染区域；
		- `GlowFilter`：物体边缘外发光光晕，依附图形轮廓。
	5. 适用场景
		- 游戏天空太阳光、窗口丁达尔光束、舞台灯光、氛围特效。

3. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>GodrayFilter 上帝光滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	    input[type="range"] {
	      flex: 1;
	    }
	    .val {
	      width: 60px;
	      text-align: right;
	      font-size: 13px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>time 时间因子</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="3" step="0.001" id="inpTime">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>gain 光线强度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="2" step="0.001" id="inpGain">
	        <span class="val">0.5</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>lacunarity 噪声密度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0.1" max="4" step="0.001" id="inpLacunarity">
	        <span class="val">1.565</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>alpha 光束透明度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.001" id="inpAlpha">
	        <span class="val">0.313</span>
	      </div>
	       <div class="row">
	        <label>center.x 光源X</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-200" max="1200" step="1" id="inpCenterX">
	        <span class="val">960</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>center.y 光源Y</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-300" max="600" step="1" id="inpCenterY">
	        <span class="val">-100</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>parallel 平行光束</label>
	        <input type="checkbox" id="chkParallel">
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>angle 光线角度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-90" max="90" step="0.01" id="inpAngle">
	        <span class="val">-20.88</span>
	      </div>
	
	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        GodrayFilter：上帝光丁达尔光束滤镜<br />
	        animating勾选后ticker自动累加time；parallel=true平行光使用angle，false放射光使用center
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);
	    const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
	    const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
	    sprite.scale.set(0.5);
	    app.stage.addChild(sprite);

	    const godrayFilter = new PIXI.filters.GodrayFilter({
	      time: 1,
	      gain: 0.9,
	      lacunarity: 1.565,
	      alpha: 0.313,
	      parallel: true,
	      angle: -20.88,
	      center: { x: 960, y: -100 }
	    });
	    sprite.filters = [godrayFilter];

	    const inpTime = document.getElementById('inpTime');
	    const inpGain = document.getElementById('inpGain');
	    const inpLacunarity = document.getElementById('inpLacunarity');
	    const inpAlpha = document.getElementById('inpAlpha');
	    const chkParallel = document.getElementById('chkParallel');
	    const inpAngle = document.getElementById('inpAngle');
	    const inpCenterX = document.getElementById('inpCenterX');
	    const inpCenterY = document.getElementById('inpCenterY');

	    const valTime = inpTime.nextElementSibling;
	    const valGain = inpGain.nextElementSibling;
	    const valLacunarity = inpLacunarity.nextElementSibling;
	    const valAlpha = inpAlpha.nextElementSibling;
	    const valAngle = inpAngle.nextElementSibling;
	    const valCenterX = inpCenterX.nextElementSibling;
	    const valCenterY = inpCenterY.nextElementSibling;

	    //初始化控件
	    inpTime.value = godrayFilter.time;
	    valTime.textContent = godrayFilter.time.toFixed(3);
	    inpGain.value = godrayFilter.gain;
	    valGain.textContent = godrayFilter.gain.toFixed(3);
	    inpLacunarity.value = godrayFilter.lacunarity;
	    valLacunarity.textContent = godrayFilter.lacunarity.toFixed(3);
	    inpAlpha.value = godrayFilter.alpha;
	    valAlpha.textContent = godrayFilter.alpha.toFixed(3);
	    chkParallel.checked = godrayFilter.parallel;
	    inpAngle.value = godrayFilter.angle;
	    valAngle.textContent = godrayFilter.angle.toFixed(2);
	    inpCenterX.value = godrayFilter.center.x;
	    valCenterX.textContent = godrayFilter.center.x;
	    inpCenterY.value = godrayFilter.center.y;
	    valCenterY.textContent = godrayFilter.center.y;

	    inpTime.oninput = () => {
	      godrayFilter.time = Number(inpTime.value);
	      valTime.textContent = godrayFilter.time.toFixed(3);
	    };
	    inpGain.oninput = () => {
	      godrayFilter.gain = Number(inpGain.value);
	      valGain.textContent = godrayFilter.gain.toFixed(3);
	    };
	    inpLacunarity.oninput = () => {
	      godrayFilter.lacunarity = Number(inpLacunarity.value);
	      valLacunarity.textContent = godrayFilter.lacunarity.toFixed(3);
	    };
	    inpAlpha.oninput = () => {
	      godrayFilter.alpha = Number(inpAlpha.value);
	      valAlpha.textContent = godrayFilter.alpha.toFixed(3);
	    };
	    chkParallel.onchange = () => {
	      godrayFilter.parallel = chkParallel.checked;
	    };
	    inpAngle.oninput = () => {
	      godrayFilter.angle = Number(inpAngle.value);
	      valAngle.textContent = godrayFilter.angle.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    inpCenterX.oninput = () => {
	      godrayFilter.center.x = Number(inpCenterX.value);
	      valCenterX.textContent = godrayFilter.center.x;
	    };
	    inpCenterY.oninput = () => {
	      godrayFilter.center.y = Number(inpCenterY.value);
	      valCenterY.textContent = godrayFilter.center.y;
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>
	```

### 6.2.21 灰度滤镜-GrayscaleFilter

> 灰度滤镜:
> 去除图像色彩信息，把彩色画面转为黑白灰度效果。gray=0 保留原图彩色，gray=1 输出完全黑白灰度图像。

1. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>GrayscaleFilter 灰度滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>启用 GrayscaleFilter</label>
	        <input type="checkbox" id="chkEnable">
	      </div>
	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        GrayscaleFilter：图像灰度滤镜<br/>
	        去除图像色彩信息，把彩色画面转为黑白灰度效果；勾选开启，取消显示原图
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);
	    const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
	    const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
	    sprite.scale.set(0.5);
	    app.stage.addChild(sprite);
	    const grayscaleFilter = new PIXI.filters.GrayscaleFilter();
	    const chkEnable = document.getElementById('chkEnable');
	    chkEnable.onchange = ()=>{
	      sprite.filters = chkEnable.checked ? [grayscaleFilter] : [];
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>
	```

2. 运行效果

### 6.2.22 HSL色彩滤镜-HslAdjustmentFilter

`HslAdjustmentFilter` 属于 **pixi‑filters**，基于HSL色彩模型对图像进行调色，独立调整色相、饱和度、亮度，支持单色着色模式与滤镜整体透明度，实现画面色彩偏移、单色染色、褪色、提亮压暗等色彩校正效果。

> 
> 原理：把图像RGB颜色转换到HSL色彩空间，分别修改hue色相、saturation饱和度、lightness亮度分量；colorize开启后强制整图使用单一色相做染色；再转换回RGB输出；alpha控制滤镜混合透明度。

1. 参数

	| 参数 | 中文 | 默认 | 说明 |
	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
	| `hue` | 色相 | `0` | 色相偏移角度，范围0‑360；数值改变画面整体色彩倾向，0为原始色相 |
	| `saturation` | 饱和度 | `0` | 色彩饱和强度；0完全灰度黑白，大于0色彩逐渐艳丽 |
	| `lightness` | 亮度 | `0` | 画面明暗；负数画面压暗，正数画面提亮，不可过度避免曝光丢失细节 |
	| `colorize` | 单色着色 | `false` | 复选开关；开启后抛弃原图颜色，使用hue指定单一色相给整张图染色 |
	| `alpha` | 滤镜透明度 | `1` | 滤镜混合透明度；0完全不生效显示原图，1完整应用滤镜效果 |

	> 
	> 性能提示

	- 该滤镜属于简单颜色矩阵变换，GPU开销极小，适合大量元素或者实时动态调色；
	- colorize开启可以快速制作旧照片、单色电影滤镜；
	- alpha可以做滤镜淡入淡出过渡动画。

2. 关键特性

	1. **完整HSL五参数调节**
		- `hue`：转动色彩相位，colorize关闭时仅偏移原有颜色；开启时作为唯一染色色相；
		- `saturation`：控制颜色浓淡，归零输出黑白画面；
		- `lightness`：相对偏移调节画面提亮压暗；
		- `colorize`：全局单色染色开关；
		- `alpha`：滤镜整体混合透明度，做效果强度过渡。
	2. 和GrayscaleFilter区别
		- `HslAdjustmentFilter`：完整HSL调色工具，可染色、可调透明度；
		- `GrayscaleFilter`：仅简单转为灰度，无染色、alpha控制。
	3. 适用场景
		- 图片调色、单色滤镜、旧照片风格、游戏角色变色、UI色彩校正、滤镜淡入动画。

3. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>HslAdjustmentFilter HSL调色滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	    input[type="range"] {
	      flex: 1;
	    }
	    .val {
	      width: 60px;
	      text-align: right;
	      font-size: 13px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>hue 色相</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="360" step="0.1" id="inpHue">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>saturation 饱和度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-1" max="2" step="0.001" id="inpSaturation">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>lightness 亮度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-1" max="1" step="0.001" id="inpLightness">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>colorize 单色着色</label>
	        <input type="checkbox" id="chkColorize">
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>alpha 滤镜透明度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.001" id="inpAlpha">
	        <span class="val">1</span>
	      </div>

	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        HslAdjustmentFilter：HSL调色滤镜<br />
	        colorize开启整张图单色染色；alpha控制滤镜混合透明度
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);
	    const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
	    const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
	    sprite.scale.set(0.5);
	    app.stage.addChild(sprite);

	    const hslFilter = new PIXI.filters.HslAdjustmentFilter({
	      hue: 0,
	      saturation: 0,
	      lightness: 0,
	      colorize: false,
	      alpha: 1
	    });
	    sprite.filters = [hslFilter];

	    const inpHue = document.getElementById('inpHue');
	    const inpSaturation = document.getElementById('inpSaturation');
	    const inpLightness = document.getElementById('inpLightness');
	    const chkColorize = document.getElementById('chkColorize');
	    const inpAlpha = document.getElementById('inpAlpha');

	    const valHue = inpHue.nextElementSibling;
	    const valSaturation = inpSaturation.nextElementSibling;
	    const valLightness = inpLightness.nextElementSibling;
	    const valAlpha = inpAlpha.nextElementSibling;

	    //初始化控件
	    inpHue.value = hslFilter.hue;
	    valHue.textContent = hslFilter.hue.toFixed(1);
	    inpSaturation.value = hslFilter.saturation;
	    valSaturation.textContent = hslFilter.saturation.toFixed(3);
	    inpLightness.value = hslFilter.lightness;
	    valLightness.textContent = hslFilter.lightness.toFixed(3);
	    chkColorize.checked = hslFilter.colorize;
	    inpAlpha.value = hslFilter.alpha;
	    valAlpha.textContent = hslFilter.alpha.toFixed(3);

	    inpHue.oninput = () => {
	      hslFilter.hue = Number(inpHue.value);
	      valHue.textContent = hslFilter.hue.toFixed(1);
	    };
	    inpSaturation.oninput = () => {
	      hslFilter.saturation = Number(inpSaturation.value);
	      valSaturation.textContent = hslFilter.saturation.toFixed(3);
	    };
	    inpLightness.oninput = () => {
	      hslFilter.lightness = Number(inpLightness.value);
	      valLightness.textContent = hslFilter.lightness.toFixed(3);
	    };
	    chkColorize.onchange = () => {
	      hslFilter.colorize = chkColorize.checked;
	    };
	    inpAlpha.oninput = () => {
	      hslFilter.alpha = Number(inpAlpha.value);
	      valAlpha.textContent = hslFilter.alpha.toFixed(3);
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>
	```

### 6.2.23 卡瓦塞模糊滤镜-KawaseBlurFilter

`KawaseBlurFilter` 属于 **pixi‑filters**，高性能卡瓦塞模糊滤镜，迭代式高斯模糊实现，适合做背景虚化、面板毛玻璃效果；可分别控制模糊强度、迭代质量、像素采样步长X/Y。

> 
> 原理：Kawase 迭代卷积模糊算法，多次对纹理进行下采样偏移采样实现模糊；strength控制模糊半径；quality为迭代次数；pixelSize.x / pixelSize.y控制水平、垂直方向像素采样尺寸，数值越大模糊越粗糙、性能越好。

1. 参数

	| 参数 | 中文 | 默认 | 说明 |
	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
	| `strength` | 模糊强度 | `4` | 模糊半径，数值越大画面越模糊；过高会产生画面溢出黑边 |
	| `quality` | 迭代质量 | `3` | 模糊迭代次数；次数越高模糊越平滑，性能开销随之上升 |
	| `pixelSize.x` | 水平像素尺寸 | `1` | 水平方向采样步长；增大会加速模糊，画面颗粒感变强 |
	| `pixelSize.y` | 垂直像素尺寸 | `1` | 垂直方向采样步长；可与x设置不同，制作单向拉伸模糊 |

	> 
	> 性能提示

	- KawaseBlur属于迭代下采样模糊，相比原生高斯模糊性能更优，适合实时页面、UI毛玻璃；
	- quality不要设置过高，推荐2‑6区间；strength过大会出现边缘黑边，需要扩大filterArea；
	- pixelSize增大可以显著降低GPU消耗，代价是模糊出现方块颗粒感。

2. 关键特性

	1. **迭代式高性能模糊**
		- `strength`：直接控制模糊的半径大小；
		- `quality`：迭代次数，提升quality让模糊过渡更加柔和；
		- `pixelSize.x / pixelSize.y`：分别控制水平、垂直采样粒度，可以实现非对称模糊。
	2. 和普通高斯模糊区别
		- `KawaseBlurFilter`：迭代下采样，大模糊半径性能友好；
		- `GaussianBlurFilter`：单遍卷积，大半径性能差。
	3. 适用场景
		- UI毛玻璃、背景虚化、弹窗模糊背景、发光柔化、图片雾化效果。
3. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>KawaseBlurFilter 卡瓦塞模糊滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	    input[type="range"] {
	      flex: 1;
	    }
	    .val {
	      width: 60px;
	      text-align: right;
	      font-size: 13px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>strength 模糊强度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0" max="16" step="0.1" id="inpStrength">
	        <span class="val">4</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>quality 迭代质量</label>
	        <input type="range" min="1" max="8" step="1" id="inpQuality">
	        <span class="val">3</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>pixelSize.x 水平像素</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0.1" max="8" step="0.01" id="inpPixelX">
	        <span class="val">1</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>pixelSize.y 垂直像素</label>
	        <input type="range" min="0.1" max="8" step="0.01" id="inpPixelY">
	        <span class="val">1</span>
	      </div>

	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        KawaseBlurFilter：卡瓦塞高性能模糊滤镜<br />
	        strength模糊半径；quality迭代次数；pixelSize控制采样粒度，数值越大性能越高画面越粗糙
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);
	    const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
	    const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
	    sprite.scale.set(0.5);
	    app.stage.addChild(sprite);

	    const blurFilter = new PIXI.filters.KawaseBlurFilter({
	      strength:4,
	      quality:3,
	      pixelSize:{x:1,y:1}
	    });
	    sprite.filters = [blurFilter];

	    const inpStrength = document.getElementById('inpStrength');
	    const inpQuality = document.getElementById('inpQuality');
	    const inpPixelX = document.getElementById('inpPixelX');
	    const inpPixelY = document.getElementById('inpPixelY');

	    const valStrength = inpStrength.nextElementSibling;
	    const valQuality = inpQuality.nextElementSibling;
	    const valPixelX = inpPixelX.nextElementSibling;
	    const valPixelY = inpPixelY.nextElementSibling;

	    //初始化控件
	    inpStrength.value = blurFilter.strength;
	    valStrength.textContent = blurFilter.strength.toFixed(1);
	    inpQuality.value = blurFilter.quality;
	    valQuality.textContent = blurFilter.quality;
	    inpPixelX.value = blurFilter.pixelSize.x;
	    valPixelX.textContent = blurFilter.pixelSize.x.toFixed(2);
	    inpPixelY.value = blurFilter.pixelSize.y;
	    valPixelY.textContent = blurFilter.pixelSize.y.toFixed(2);

	    inpStrength.oninput = () => {
	      blurFilter.strength = Number(inpStrength.value);
	      valStrength.textContent = blurFilter.strength.toFixed(1);
	    };
	    inpQuality.oninput = () => {
	      blurFilter.quality = Number(inpQuality.value);
	      valQuality.textContent = blurFilter.quality;
	    };
	    inpPixelX.oninput = () => {
	      blurFilter.pixelSize.x = Number(inpPixelX.value);
	      valPixelX.textContent = blurFilter.pixelSize.x.toFixed(2);
	    };
	    inpPixelY.oninput = () => {
	      blurFilter.pixelSize.y = Number(inpPixelY.value);
	      valPixelY.textContent = blurFilter.pixelSize.y.toFixed(2);
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>
	```

### 6.2.24 运动模糊滤镜-MotionBlurFilter

`MotionBlurFilter` 属于 **pixi‑filters**，运动模糊滤镜，基于速度向量实现双向非对称运动拖影，支持独立X/Y运动速度、采样核大小、偏移量。

> 
> 原理：使用 `velocity.x / velocity.y` 二维速度向量定义运动方向与拖影强度，不再使用角度；沿速度向量做多点采样叠加；`kernelSize` 采样点数量控制平滑度；`offset` 整体偏移拖影中心，实现残影前后偏移。

1. 参数

	| 参数 | 中文 | 默认 | 说明 |
	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
	| `velocity.x` | X轴运动速度 | `40` | 水平方向拖影，正值向右，负值向左，数值越大拖影越长 |
	| `velocity.y` | Y轴运动速度 | `40` | 垂直方向拖影，正值向下，负值向上，数值越大拖影越长 |
	| `kernelSize` | 采样核大小 | `15` | 采样点数量，越大拖影过渡越平滑，GPU开销上升，必须为奇数 |
	| `offset` | 拖影偏移 | `0` | 偏移采样中心；0居中；正数向运动方向前移；负数向后拖尾 |

	> 
	> 性能提示

	- `kernelSize` 建议 5‑21，尽量使用奇数；数值过高会严重降帧；
	- `velocity.x/y` 控制拖影长度，不要设置极端大值，容易出现画面边缘撕裂；
	- 做物体动画时，每一帧把物体移动量赋值给 velocity，可得到物理匹配的真实运动模糊；
	- offset!=0 可以做出“向前冲”或者“向后拖残影”的视觉效果。

2. 关键特性

	1. **二维速度向量运动模糊**
		- `velocity.x / velocity.y`：独立控制水平、垂直拖影，支持斜向、单向运动；
		- `kernelSize`：采样点数，决定拖影平滑程度；
		- `offset`：调整拖影重心，改变残影分布。
	2. 和 KawaseBlurFilter 区别
		- `MotionBlurFilter`：定向矢量拖影，模拟物体运动速度感；
		- `KawaseBlurFilter`：全向均匀虚化，毛玻璃/背景模糊。
	3. 适用场景
		- 高速移动物体、镜头震动、冲刺冲刺特效、粒子拖尾、动画帧同步运动模糊。
3. 代码示例

	```html
	<!DOCTYPE html>
	<html lang="zh-CN">
	<head>
	  <meta charset="UTF-8">
	  <title>MotionBlurFilter 运动模糊滤镜</title>
	  <script src="./js/pixi.js"></script>
	  <script src="./js/pixi-filters.js"></script>
	  <style>
	    .wrap {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 20px;
	      align-items: flex-start;
	    }
	    .panel {
	      width: 320px;
	      display: flex;
	      flex-direction: column;
	      gap: 12px;
	      border: 1px solid #ccc;
	      padding: 14px;
	      border-radius: 6px;
	    }
	    .row {
	      display: flex;
	      gap: 8px;
	      align-items: center;
	    }
	    label {
	      width: 140px;
	      font-size: 14px;
	    }
	    input[type="range"] {
	      flex: 1;
	    }
	    .val {
	      width: 60px;
	      text-align: right;
	      font-size: 13px;
	    }
	  </style>
	</head>
	<body>
	  <div class="wrap">
	    <div id="canvasWrap"></div>
	    <div class="panel">
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>velocity.x X速度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-100" max="100" step="0.1" id="inpVelX">
	        <span class="val">40</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>velocity.y Y速度</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-100" max="100" step="0.1" id="inpVelY">
	        <span class="val">40</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>kernelSize 采样核</label>
	        <input type="range" min="3" max="21" step="2" id="inpKernelSize">
	        <span class="val">15</span>
	      </div>
	      <div class="row">
	        <label>offset 拖影偏移</label>
	        <input type="range" min="-1" max="1" step="0.01" id="inpOffset">
	        <span class="val">0</span>
	      </div>

	      <div style="font-size:12px;color:#555;">
	        MotionBlurFilter：矢量运动模糊<br />
	        velocity.x/y控制双向拖影；kernelSize为奇数；offset调整残影重心
	      </div>
	    </div>
	  </div>
	  <script type="module">
	    const app = new PIXI.Application();
	    await app.init({ width: 345, height: 460 });
	    document.querySelector('#canvasWrap').appendChild(app.canvas);
	    const tex = await PIXI.Assets.load('./image/01.jpg');
	    const sprite = PIXI.Sprite.from(tex);
	    sprite.scale.set(0.5);
	    app.stage.addChild(sprite);

	    const motionBlurFilter = new PIXI.filters.MotionBlurFilter({
	      velocity: { x:40, y:40 },
	      kernelSize: 15,
	      offset: 0
	    });
	    sprite.filters = [motionBlurFilter];

	    const inpVelX = document.getElementById('inpVelX');
	    const inpVelY = document.getElementById('inpVelY');
	    const inpKernelSize = document.getElementById('inpKernelSize');
	    const inpOffset = document.getElementById('inpOffset');

	    const valVelX = inpVelX.nextElementSibling;
	    const valVelY = inpVelY.nextElementSibling;
	    const valKernelSize = inpKernelSize.nextElementSibling;
	    const valOffset = inpOffset.nextElementSibling;

	    //初始化控件
	    inpVelX.value = motionBlurFilter.velocity.x;
	    valVelX.textContent = motionBlurFilter.velocity.x.toFixed(1);
	    inpVelY.value = motionBlurFilter.velocity.y;
	    valVelY.textContent = motionBlurFilter.velocity.y.toFixed(1);
	    inpKernelSize.value = motionBlurFilter.kernelSize;
	    valKernelSize.textContent = motionBlurFilter.kernelSize;
	    inpOffset.value = motionBlurFilter.offset;
	    valOffset.textContent = motionBlurFilter.offset.toFixed(2);

	    inpVelX.oninput = () => {
	      motionBlurFilter.velocity.x = Number(inpVelX.value);
	      valVelX.textContent = motionBlurFilter.velocity.x.toFixed(1);
	    };
	    inpVelY.oninput = () => {
	      motionBlurFilter.velocity.y = Number(inpVelY.value);
	      valVelY.textContent = motionBlurFilter.velocity.y.toFixed(1);
	    };
	    inpKernelSize.oninput = () => {
	      motionBlurFilter.kernelSize = Number(inpKernelSize.value);
	      valKernelSize.textContent = motionBlurFilter.kernelSize;
	    };
	    inpOffset.oninput = () => {
	      motionBlurFilter.offset = Number(inpOffset.value);
	      valOffset.textContent = motionBlurFilter.offset.toFixed(2);
	    };
	  </script>
	</body>
	</html>
	```
